Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the enzymes framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 214. The resulting operator achieves bound O(732/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 214 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the enzymes structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 732. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
